Method: 论文定义了不稳定复杂度k——特征值中落在实稳定区间之外且不快速衰减的特征值个数，加上非半单模态的数量。针对这一低复杂度系统，提出了一个统一的在线算法，可处理任意LDS（包括不可对角化、具有复数或爆炸模态的系统），其可学习参数数量为Õ(k)。同时给出了基于滤波器的预测器所需滤波器数量的下界证明。

Result: 理论上证明了：1）高不稳定复杂度的系统无法在不使用指数级大控制量的情况下被稳定；2）所提算法在低k系统中实现亚线性遗憾且参数数为Õ(k)；3）任何基于滤波器的预测器至少需要k个滤波器。实验上，在高维系统中，该方法在相同参数预算下显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 不稳定复杂度k是衡量LDS在线预测难度的有效指标，当k较小时预测有意义且可实现高效算法，而高k系统本质上难以稳定。所提算法为低不稳定复杂度的LDS在线预测提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge of stabilizing a general unknown linear dynamical system (LDS) from observations, we study the natural prerequisite of online prediction. Our goal is to achieve sublinear regret with a memory footprint that adapts to the intrinsic complexity of the dynamics rather than the full hidden -- state dimension. We focus on the practically central regime of systems with low instability complexity -- eigenvalues outside the real stable interval that do not decay rapidly, together with non-semisimple modes-potentially embedded in an otherwise stable real spectrum of much higher dimension; we write $k$ for this count. This regime is the primary setting in which stabilization is plausible: we show that many systems with high instability complexity cannot be stabilized without exponentially large controls. Thus, prediction is meaningful for stabilization precisely when the instability complexity is small. Within this regime, we introduce a unified online algorithm that handles every LDS (including non-diagonalizable systems with complex or exploding modes) with a learnable parameter count of $\widetilde{O}(k)$. Finally, we prove a lower bound showing that $k$ is a valid complexity measure: any filter-based predictor needs at least $k$ filters. Experiments corroborate our theory: on a high-dimensional system, our predictor sharply outperforms prior methods at an equal parameter budget.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond the Performance Illusion: Structure-Aware Stratified Partitioning and Curriculum Distributionally Robust Optimization for Spatially Correlated Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02055)
*Prathamesh Patil,Arpit Jain,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在时空相关领域（如航空监测、精准农业、医学影像）中，随机数据集划分会导致数据泄露和隐藏分层问题，并提出统一框架：SASP（结构感知分层划分）减少时空泄露，CDRO（课程分布鲁棒优化）稳定训练，实验结果验证了该方法在泛化性、校准性和暴露隐藏失败模式上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI性能评估假设随机划分能产生独立同分布的子集，但在时空相关数据中这一假设失效，导致性能评估膨胀和少数子群体错误被掩盖。

Method: 提出SASP方法构建验证集划分以减少时空泄漏并保持类别平衡，以及CDRO方法作为分布鲁棒训练的课程式松弛以稳定优化过程。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法实现了更优的泛化性能、更可靠的置信度校准，并暴露了传统随机划分下隐藏的失败模式。

Conclusion: SASP和CDRO的组合框架能有效解决时空相关数据中的评估偏差和训练不稳定问题，为相关领域提供了更可靠的评估与训练方案。

Abstract: Performance evaluation in AI systems commonly assumes that random dataset splits produce independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) subsets. We show that this assumption often breaks down in spatiotemporally correlated domains such as aerial surveillance, precision agriculture, and medical imaging, leading to two systematic failures: data leakage, where correlated samples span training and validation splits and inflate performance estimates, and hidden stratification, where errors on minority subpopulations are obscured by aggregate metrics. To address these issues, we propose a unified evaluation and training framework for spatially correlated data. We introduce Structure-Aware Stratified Partitioning (SASP), which constructs validation splits that reduce spatiotemporal leakage while preserving meaningful class balance, and Curriculum Distributionally Robust Optimization (CDRO), a curriculum-based relaxation of distributionally robust training that stabilizes optimization under these stricter splits. Across multiple benchmarks, this combination yields consistently improved generalization, more reliable confidence calibration, and exposes failure modes that remain hidden under conventional random-split evaluation.

</details>


### [74] [SA-HGNN: Sample-Adaptive Hyperbolic Graph Neural Network for EEG-Based Depression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02063)
*Yang Li,Pan Hu,Yan Zhang,Wenfan Yang,Tao Wu,Lianbo Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出样本自适应双曲图神经网络(SA-HGNN)用于抑郁症脑电信号识别，通过动态构建大脑网络拓扑、双曲图卷积捕获层次结构以及注意力池化降噪，在公共数据集上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症患者大脑网络功能连接具有固有的层次结构，现有GNN方法难以准确捕获这种真实的连接模式，需要一种能够适应样本差异并挖掘层次拓扑关系的模型。

Method: SA-HGNN包含三个核心模块：1) 样本自适应图构建模块动态生成个性化大脑网络拓扑；2) 双曲图卷积利用双曲几何克服欧氏空间表示瓶颈，精确捕获潜在层次关系；3) 注意力池化模块自适应滤除EEG信号中的高冗余噪声通道。

Result: 在公共EEG数据集上，该方法在静息态和任务态范式中均展现出优越性能，验证了对噪声的鲁棒性以及捕获抑郁症患者大脑网络异常功能连接模式的有效性。

Conclusion: SA-HGNN通过双曲几何与样本自适应机制有效解决了抑郁症脑网络层次结构建模难题，为基于EEG的抑郁症识别提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been widely used to capture spatial functional connectivity patterns to improve electroencephalography (EEG)-based depression recognition performance. However, the functional connectivity of brain networks in patients with depression exhibits an inherent hierarchical structure, making it difficult to capture accurate connection patterns. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel model named Sample-Adaptive Hyperbolic Graph Neural Network (SA-HGNN), which aims to accurately extract the authentic hierarchical structure of depression-affected brain networks. Specifically, the proposed model comprises three core modules. First, a Sample-Adaptive Graph Construction module dynamically constructs personalized brain network topologies to capture more complex spatial relationships within the brain network. Second, hyperbolic graph convolution is employed to overcome the representation bottlenecks of Euclidean space, leveraging hyperbolic geometry to precisely capture latent hierarchical relationships within the brain network. Finally, an Attention Pooling module adaptively filters out highly redundant noise channels in EEG signals, effectively mitigating the interference of inherent noise on the authentic hierarchical topology. Extensive experiments on public EEG datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method across resting-state and task-related paradigms, validating its robustness to noise and efficacy in capturing abnormal functional connectivity patterns in brain networks of patients with depression.

</details>


### [75] [kNNGuard: Turning LLM Hidden Activations into a Training-Free Configurable Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02072)
*Mahmoud Abdelfattah,Hamid Nasiri,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: kNNGuard是一种无需训练的LLM护栏方法，利用现成LLM的激活空间，通过少量样本(50个)和多层kNN融合分类，在多个领域达到或超越微调方法，速度提升2.7-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微调的护栏方法存在泛化能力差、推理延迟高的问题，亟需一种高效、低延迟且无需训练的替代方案。

Method: kNNGuard利用现成LLM的激活空间，提取少量(50个)安全/不安全提示的隐藏激活，通过多层kNN融合激活空间和嵌入空间分数进行分类。

Result: 在涵盖主题和安全提示的六个领域中，kNNGuard的F1分数与微调SOTA护栏相当或更优；推理速度比最佳可比护栏快2.7倍，比微调安全分类器快10倍；领域适配仅需不到10秒构建标注库。

Conclusion: kNNGuard作为一种无需训练、低延迟、可配置的护栏方案，在多个领域表现出竞争力，能够快速适配新领域并集成到生产LLM管线中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in domains requiring guardrails to detect unsafe, off-topic, or adversarial prompts. Existing guardrails predominately rely on fine-tuning to build classifiers, which often suffer from low generalization and high inference latency. We present kNNGuard, a training-free guardrail that utilizes the activation space of an off-the-shelf LLM. Given a small bank of 50 safe and unsafe prompts, kNNGuard extracts hidden activations and performs multi-layer kNN fusing activation-space and embedding-space scores for classification. Across six domains spanning topical and security prompts, kNNGuard achieves competitive or superior F1 compared to fine-tuned state-of-the-art guardrails while running 2.7x faster than the best comparable guardrail, and 10x faster than a fine-tuned safety classifier without gradient updates or fine-tuning. Domain adaptation requires only updating the labeled bank, which can be constructed in under 10 seconds and several orders of magnitude faster than established guardrails. We also analyze the impact of system prompts, layer selection, and integration into production LLM pipelines as a configurable, low-latency guardrail.

</details>


### [76] [Fourier Neural Operators for Rayleigh-Bénard Convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02088)
*Chelsea Maria John,Thibaut Lunet,Sebastian Götschel,Andreas Herten,Stefan Kesselheim,Daniel Ruprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），通过预测时间增量而非完整解来建模二维瑞利-贝纳德对流，在保持紧凑模型大小（314k参数，1.26 MB）和快速推理（7 ms）的同时，相比标准FNO基线实现了更高精度。实验表明FNO可泛化到更细网格，但精度受限于训练数据分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高FNO对二维瑞利-贝纳德对流的建模精度，同时保持模型的高效性和紧凑性。

Method: 改进标准FNO，采用预测时间增量（而非完整解）的策略，构建紧凑的网络架构（314k参数）。

Result: 模型参数仅314k，大小1.26 MB，推理时间7 ms，精度优于标准FNO基线；FNO可泛化至更细网格，但精度受训练数据分辨率限制。

Conclusion: 增量预测策略能有效提升FNO在瑞利-贝纳德对流建模中的性能，模型的泛化能力受限于训练数据分辨率。

Abstract: We propose an improved Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) for modeling two-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard convection by predicting time increments instead of full solutions, achieving higher accuracy than a standard FNO baseline. The resulting model is compact (314k parameters, 1.26 MB) and fast (7 ms inference), while maintaining similar accuracy as demonstrated in previous benchmarks. We show that although FNOs generalize to finer meshes, accuracy remains limited by the resolution of the training data.

</details>


### [77] [Ask the Right Comparison:Bias-Aware Bayesian Active Top-$k$ Ranking with LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02104)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯推断框架，通过在潜在质量模型中显式建模判断偏差（如冗长性、位置偏好），并结合Top-k感知的主动获取策略，在固定比较预算下更准确地识别Top-k项目。实验表明，该方法能有效纠正廉价/中端LLM判断者的系统性偏差，将召回率从0.5-0.6提升至0.84-1.0，而对前沿模型影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者存在噪声和系统性偏差（如偏好冗长、格式好的回答及位置效应），简单聚合投票只能恢复展示顺序的排名而非真实质量排名。现有方法未能在有限比较预算下有效纠正这些偏差以识别Top-k项目。

Method: 1) 将评判建模为潜在质量上的贝叶斯推断，加入显式的判断偏差协变量（冗长性、位置），并通过收缩先验正则化。2) 提出Top-k感知的主动获取规则，选择能最大程度减少Top-k成员不确定性（而非完整排名不确定性）的下一次比较。

Result: 在16个真实LLM（Llama、Qwen、Phi-4、GPT-4o-mini/5.1/5.5、Gemini、DeepSeek、Claude等）的基准测试中，朴素聚合在偏差判断者上无论预算多少都无法正确识别Top-k，而偏差感知模型能恢复；Top-k感知获取相比轮询或全局不确定性（D-最优）规则，用更少比较达到天花板。廉价/中端模型存在强冗长偏差，经模型纠正后召回率从0.5-0.6提升至0.84-1.0；前沿模型偏差小，偏差感知建模改变不大。

Conclusion: 偏差是真实存在且异构的，依赖模型能力：偏差感知建模对廉价/中端判断者至关重要，能显著提升Top-k识别准确率；对前沿判断者则非必需。Top-k感知的主动获取策略在预算有限时比传统策略更高效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as cheap, scalable judges that compare candidate outputs pairwise -- to rank responses, select models, or triage papers. Yet LLM judges are both noisy and systematically biased: they favor verbose or well-formatted answers and exhibit position effects, so simply aggregating their votes recovers a ranking of presentation, not of true quality. We study the practical goal of identifying the \topk{} items under a fixed comparison budget, and make two contributions. First, we cast judging as Bayesian inference over latent quality with explicit, judge-specific bias covariates (verbosity, position), regularized by a shrinkage prior so that the data decide which biases a given judge actually exhibits. Second, we introduce a \topk-aware active acquisition rule that chooses the next comparison to maximally reduce uncertainty about \topk{} \emph{membership}, rather than about the full ranking. On a controlled benchmark with known ground-truth quality, judged by sixteen real LLMs spanning open and proprietary families (Llama, Qwen, Phi-4, GPT-4o-mini/5.1/5.5, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Claude Haiku/Sonnet/Opus), naive aggregation plateaus at a wrong \topk{} on biased judges regardless of budget, while our bias-aware model recovers it; \topk-aware acquisition reaches this ceiling with far fewer comparisons than round-robin or a global-uncertainty (D-optimal) rule. Bias is real but heterogeneous and capability-dependent: cheap and mid-tier judges carry a strong verbosity bias that our model corrects (lifting recall from $\sim$$0.5$--$0.6$ to $0.84$--$1.0$), whereas the frontier judges we tested show little bias and already rank accurately, so bias-aware modeling changes little there.

</details>


### [78] [Predictive Conformal Slip Monitoring: An Empirical Evaluation of Rolling Split Conformal Prediction for Pre-Incident Traction Loss Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02124)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了滚动分裂共形预测（Rolling Split Conformal Prediction）作为牵引力丧失前预警信号的可行性，通过监测每名车手随机森林滑移行为模型的非共形残差波动性。在19名车手、55,563个遥测样本的真实赛事数据上，该方法精度和召回率均为0.0，假阳性率高达15.3%，结果是否定的。残差自相关诊断表明分裂共形的可交换性假设对所有车手均不成立，这是高误报率的原因之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的牵引力控制架构只能在轮胎附着极限已被突破后介入，缺乏基于统计的提前预警机制。作者希望探索一种在牵引力完全丧失前就能发出预警信号的方法，从而为车辆动力学安全提供更前置的干预窗口。

Method: 使用滚动分裂共形预测，监测每名车手专属随机森林期望滑移行为模型的非共形残差波动性；修正了滑移代理中的混淆（将车速作为显式特征加入模型）；使用每名车手的所有比赛圈而非仅最快圈；以FIA赛事控制消息和赛道限制圈数删除记录的真实时间戳事件标签作为评估基准。

Result: 在19名车手、55,563个测试阶段遥测样本上，滚动波动性检测器对14个地面真实事件的平均精度=0.0，平均召回率=0.0；约15.3%的样本被标记为异常，假阳性率过高；静态95百分位阈值基线在性能上同样不佳，且不具复杂性优势。所有19名车手的残差Ljung-Box检验p<0.001，表明可交换性假设被违反。

Conclusion: 该方法在实践中不可行，高假阳性率和零召回率使其无法作为早期预警信号使用；共形预测的可交换性假设在赛车遥测数据的时间序列结构中不成立，是失败的核心原因；作者报告了一个方法严谨的否定性发现，诊断了原因，并勾勒了实现真正预测性版本所需的条件。

Abstract: Conventional traction control architectures intervene only after the adhesion limit of a tire has already been breached. This paper investigates whether Rolling Split Conformal Prediction , monitoring the volatility of non-conformity residuals from a per-driver Random Forest model of expected slip behavior , can serve as a statistically grounded pre-incident warning signal, ahead of gross traction loss. Unlike an earlier internal draft of this work, the evaluation reported here corrects a confound in the slip proxy (vehicle speed is included as an explicit model feature, not left implicit in the target's denominator), uses every racing lap for each driver rather than only the fastest lap, and is scored against real, timestamped incident labels extracted from FIA Race Control Messages and track-limits lap deletions rather than narrated post-hoc. The result is negative: across 19 drivers and 55,563 test-phase telemetry samples, the rolling-volatility detector achieves a mean precision of essentially 0.0 and mean recall of 0.0 against 14 ground-truth incidents, while flagging on average 15.3% of all samples as anomalous , too high a false-alarm rate for any early-warning use. A static 95th-percentile threshold baseline performs no better in any way that would justify the added complexity of the conformal-volatility formulation. Residual autocorrelation diagnostics show the split-conformal exchangeability assumption is violated for every driver (Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n = 19/19), which is one plausible driver of the high false-alarm rate. We report this as a methodologically rigorous negative finding, diagnose its likely causes, and outline what a genuinely predictive version of this approach would require.

</details>


### [79] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种基于连续时间控制和强化学习的扩散采样自适应时间步分配方法，可在不改变采样流水线其余部分的情况下，通过学习的时间扭曲函数实现自适应时间步长，显著提升样本质量并具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散采样时间步分配方法（如均匀调度和手工设计调度）依赖于固定预设，往往不是最优的，限制了采样效率和质量。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间）将时间分配建模为连续时间控制问题，以采样时钟速度作为控制量；进一步引入ART-RL，通过高斯策略将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，证明两者在优化器层面等价，并推导出可实现的Actor-Critic更新规则。

Result: 在低维控制和图像生成实验中，ART-RL仅通过改变时间步网格即可融入现有扩散采样器，在相同计算预算下持续优于强基线调度；学习的调度策略可在不同采样预算、数据集、求解器、流水线和表示空间之间泛化，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: ART-RL提供了一种即插即用的自适应时间步分配方案，可显著提升扩散采样样本质量，且具有出色的跨设定泛化能力。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [80] [Probing Chemical Language Models: Effects of Pre-training and Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02140)
*Anna Karnysheva,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了化学语言模型（CLMs）对78种分子子结构的编码能力，发现预训练能提升模型对分子结构的感知能力（尤其是高层），随机初始化模型的第一层已能良好编码环状结构，且微调对任务相关子结构的影响更大，符合化学理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学语言模型使用SMILES等线性化表示训练，但尚不清楚它们编码了哪些有化学意义的子结构，需要系统性地理解CLMs的内部表征。

Method: 对8个预训练和6个随机初始化的CLMs进行78种分子子结构的探针分析，并研究微调对分子子结构表征的影响。

Result: 预训练提升了CLMs的分子结构感知能力（尤其是高层）；随机初始化模型的第一层已能良好编码环状结构；微调对任务相关子结构的影响更大。

Conclusion: 预训练能增强CLMs对分子子结构的编码能力，微调对表征的改变遵循化学理论，即任务相关的子结构变化更显著。

Abstract: Chemical language models (CLMs) are trained with linearized representations such as SMILES, yet it remains unclear which chemically meaningful substructures they encode. To foster a better understanding of CLMs, we conduct a systematic study and probe for 78 molecular substructures across eight pre-trained and six randomly initialized models. We furthermore study how fine-tuning on chemical downstream tasks affects the learned representations of molecular substructures. Our results show that pre-training generally improves molecular structure awareness of CLMs, particularly in the upper layers. Moreover, randomly initialized models already encode ring structures well in the first layer. Our analysis on two chemical downstream tasks further reveals that, interestingly, fine-tuning affects task-relevant molecular substructures more than others, indicating that the changes in the representations follow chemical theory.

</details>


### [81] [Predicting Early Stages Of Alzheimer's Disease And Identifying Key Biomarkers Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02142)
*Debopriya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习和人工神经网络的阿尔茨海默病早期检测模型，通过迭代插补处理缺失值、Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类别不平衡、包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择，并构建基于逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM的堆叠集成模型，在ADNI数据集上评估性能，旨在寻找最佳分类器及重要生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期症状轻微，易被误认为正常衰老，导致多数患者确诊时已处于晚期。目前尚无治愈方法，但早期检测有助于医生更好地管理病情并及时干预，因此亟需有效的早期诊断工具。

Method: 使用ADNI数据集，采用迭代插补处理缺失值、Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类别不平衡、包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择，构建堆叠集成模型（基分类器：逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN、LightGBM）以及人工神经网络，通过精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC评估性能。

Result: 堆叠集成模型与人工神经网络在ADNI数据集上进行了性能比较，但摘要中未给出具体的数值结果。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在找出最佳分类器并识别出有助于早期诊断阿尔茨海默病的重要生物标志物，为临床早期干预提供支持。

Abstract: Alzheimers disease (AD) is a brain disorder that develops slowly and mainly affects memory, thinking, language, and daily activities. It is one of the most common causes of dementia and creates many difficulties for patients as well as their families. In the early stage, the symptoms are often mild and may look like normal ageing. For this reason, many people are diagnosed late, when the disease has already progressed. At present, there is no complete cure for AD. Still, early detection can help doctors manage the condition better and take suitable steps at the right time. In this study, a machine learning model is proposed to detect the early stages of Alzheimers disease using clinical details, neuropsychological test scores, and neuroimaging-related measures. The data used in this work is collected from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI). As the dataset has missing values, iterative imputation is applied to fill them. The dataset also has class imbalance, which is handled using Borderline SVM-SMOTE. After that, feature selection is carried out using wrapper-based and embedded methods so that only important features are used for training. The selected features are divided into training and testing sets, and feature scaling is applied. A stacking ensemble model is developed using Logistic Regression, Extra Trees, Bagging KNN, and LightGBM as base classifiers. Along with this, an artificial neural network is also trained on the same dataset. The performance of these models is compared using precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC-ROC. This study aims to find the best classifier and also identify important biomarkers that may help in the early diagnosis of Alzheimers disease.

</details>


### [82] [Dynamic Neural Graph Encoding of Inference Processes in Deep Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02166)
*Di Wu,Huan Liu,Zhixiang Chi,Yuanhao Yu,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态图神经网络编码器(DNG-Encoder)处理神经网络权重空间，通过将网络参数建模为动态图来保留推理的时序特性，在INR分类任务上超越SOTA约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法处理高维权重空间时忽略了神经网络逐层处理的时序特性，难以高效处理权重空间数据。

Method: 利用动态图表示神经网络参数以捕获推理的时序动态，设计DNG-Encoder处理这些图结构；进一步提出INR2JLS将隐式神经表示映射到联合隐空间以支持下游应用。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-INR数据集上，INR分类准确率超越SOTA约10%。

Conclusion: 通过保留网络推理的时序特性，动态图方法在权重空间分析和INR分类等任务上展现出显著优势。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in using neural networks as implicit data representations have attracted significant interest in developing machine learning methods that analyze and process the weight spaces of other neural networks. However, efficiently handling these highdimensional weight spaces remains challenging. Existing methods often overlook the sequential nature of layer-by-layer processing in neural network inference. In this work, we propose a novel approach using dynamic graphs to represent neural network parameters, capturing the temporal dynamics of inference. Our Dynamic Neural Graph Encoder (DNG-Encoder) processes these graphs, preserving the sequential nature of neural processing. Additionally, we also leverage DNG-Encoder to develop INR2JLS (Implicit Neural Representation to Joint Latent Space) for facilitate downstream applications, such as classifying Implicit Neural Representations (INRs). Our approach demonstrates significant improvements across multiple tasks, surpassing the state-of-the-art INR classification accuracy by approximately 10% on the CIFAR-100-INR.

</details>


### [83] [Bayesian Sparse Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Model Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02182)
*Jijie Zhang,Zhe Ren,Quan Zhang,Dandan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DALorRA，一种贝叶斯变分稀疏框架，通过在LoRA的秩维度上施加随机掩码来实现模型校准，在不牺牲推理精度的前提下有效缓解大语言模型微调中的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在任务特定微调中普遍存在过度自信问题，严重阻碍了可信部署，现有方法缺乏有效的校准手段。

Method: 提出DALorRA（Data-Adaptive Lower-Rank Adaptation），一种变分贝叶斯稀疏框架：将不确定性量化从密集参数空间转移到LoRA的轻量级秩级别，通过在秩维度上施加随机掩码实现贝叶斯正则化（训练时）和集成式校准（推理时）。

Result: 大量实验表明DALorRA在保持推理精度的前提下，显著提升了大语言模型的校准性能。

Conclusion: DALorRA通过秩级别的贝叶斯稀疏化有效解决了LLM微调中的过度自信问题，为可信部署提供了简单有效的方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, but their task-specific fine-tuning is notoriously plagued by overconfidence, severely hindering trustworthy deployment. We propose Data-Adaptive Lower-Rank Adaptation (DALorRA), a simple and effective variational Bayesian sparse framework that shifts the paradigm of uncertainty quantification from the dense parameter space to the lightweight rank level of low-rank adaptation (LoRA). With the insight that LoRA essentially aggregates multiple rank-one components that may provide superfluous model capacity, DALorRA imposes stochastic masking on rank dimensions, enabling Bayesian regularization of model capacity during training and ensemble-like calibration during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate DALorRA's excellent calibration of LLMs without compromising reasoning accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [Privacy-Preserving and Verifiable Approximate Distributed Coded Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02187)
*Xavier Martínez-Luaña,Alba Gude-Santos,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模型无关的对抗性分布式学习框架，通过结合GPBACC隐私增强编码计算与鲁棒聚合（联邦学习）及近似解码比较与群组测试（去中心化学习），在统一框架中同时解决隐私保护和恶意行为防御问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法通常孤立地处理隐私泄露或恶意操纵威胁，且针对特定学习范式或模型架构设计，缺乏一个能统一应对联邦学习和去中心化学习中不同对抗面的框架。

Method: 提出模型无关的统一框架，结合GPBACC隐私增强编码计算技术；在联邦学习中集成鲁棒聚合策略抵御恶意参与者，在去中心化学习中采用近似解码比较和群组测试技术实现轻量级验证和攻击者隔离，无需依赖可信聚合器。

Result: 通过攻击驱动的显式分析，实现代表性隐私攻击和恶意行为，实验表明GPBACC与鲁棒聚合及验证机制的结合能显著降低隐私泄露并提升对主动对抗者的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 隐私增强编码计算与适当的对抗防御策略相结合，能为安全分布式机器学习提供实用且可部署的基础。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing data, but it also exposes learning processes to privacy leakage and malicious manipulation. Existing defenses typically address these threats in isolation and are often tailored to specific learning paradigms or model architectures, limiting their applicability in realistic deployments. In particular, federated learning and decentralized learning exhibit distinct adversarial surfaces that are rarely addressed within a unified framework. In this paper, we present a model-agnostic framework for adversary-resistant distributed learning that jointly addresses privacy preservation and malicious behavior across both federated and decentralized settings. Our approach combines paradigm-specific defense mechanisms with GPBACC, a privacy-enhancing coded computing technique applicable to arbitrary machine learning models. For federated learning, we integrate robust aggregation strategies to mitigate the impact of malicious participants, while for decentralized learning we employ approximate decode-and-compare and group testing techniques to enable lightweight verification and adversary isolation without relying on a trusted aggregator. Crucially, we evaluate the proposed framework through an explicit, attack-driven analysis. We implement representative privacy attacks and malicious behaviors, and empirically demonstrate that the combination of GPBACC with robust aggregation and verification mechanisms significantly reduces privacy leakage and improves resilience against active adversaries. These results suggest that privacy-enhancing coded computing, when combined with appropriate adversary-resistance strategies, provides a practical and deployable foundation for secure distributed machine learning.

</details>


### [85] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSGNAR（双草图高斯-牛顿自适应比率优化框架），通过解决物理信息神经网络（PINNs）训练中因损失曲面严重病态导致的优化难题，实现了前所未有的精度和速度，在多种偏微分方程问题上将相对ℓ2误差降至双精度浮点极限（3×10⁻¹⁶），比现有方法提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在求解偏微分方程时难以达到经典数值求解器的精度，其根本障碍在于损失曲面的严重病态性导致的优化困难。现有方法无法有效克服这一优化瓶颈。

Method: DSGNAR采用双草图高斯-牛顿（doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton）模型，结合一种精心控制正则化与步长的新策略，构成可扩展的二阶优化框架。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维和Navier-Stokes等一系列问题上，DSGNAR将相对ℓ2误差降至双精度下的3×10⁻¹⁶；在经典Burgers方程上比当代结果提升五个数量级，在高维泊松问题上提升八个数量级；单精度下Burgers方程可在十秒内达到ℓ2_rel=4.75×10⁻⁷。框架对网络架构、算术精度和初始超参数具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过有效解决PINNs的病态优化问题，首次使PINNs达到甚至超越经典数值求解器的精度，且速度更快、鲁棒性更强，为物理信息神经网络的实际应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [86] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究连续奖励和消耗量下的在线资源分配问题，通过活跃加权质量指数p刻画问题难度，并提出一种样本路径边际策略，在p>1时达到T^{1/2-1/(2p)}量级遗憾下界，p=1时达到O((log T)^2)遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线资源分配研究通常假设奖励和消耗量为离散或满足非退化流体松弛条件，缺乏对连续分布下一般性问题的理解，特别是当流体松弛退化时的遗憾界。

Method: 定义活跃加权质量指数p量化价值-大小比靠近截止阈值处的请求质量；设计样本路径边际策略；通过信息论下界和策略上界分析建立遗憾的紧刻画。

Result: p>1时，遗憾下界为Ω(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})，策略匹配该下界至多对数因子；p=1时策略达到O((log T)^2)遗憾；无需流体非退化假设，允许原退化和对偶非唯一性。

Conclusion: 首次在连续奖励和消耗量的在线资源分配中，不依赖流体非退化假设，建立了依赖于活跃加权质量指数p的完整遗憾界理论，统一了不同分布下的算法性能刻画。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [87] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自解释算子学习框架，通过将算子学习重构为积分方程表示的广义函数线性模型的线性组合，并利用积分方程的可加分解性对输入域进行子区域划分，计算局部积分以评估各区域对预测的贡献，从而在流体力学问题（如血流和非定常气动力学）中实现对函数到标量/函数映射的直接可解释性。结果表明算子优先关注特征梯度大的区域，且解释性内置于算子结构本身，无需外部工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的算子学习模型（如DeepONet、FNO）虽在物理系统建模中表现优异，但其黑箱特性导致预测推理过程不透明，缺乏可解释性，难以在关键科学应用中建立信任。

Method: 将算子学习重构为广义函数线性模型（GFLM）的线性组合，通过积分方程表达；利用积分方程的可加分解性，将输入域划分为子区域并计算局部积分，量化各区域对预测的贡献，实现输入-输出区域间的直接可解释链接。

Result: 在血流动力学和非定常气动力学问题的函数到标量/函数映射任务上，模型解释了哪些输入区域驱动预测，结果显示算子优先关注特征梯度大的区域，与后验解释方法（如梯度类方法）定性一致。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了数学透明且物理可解释的算子学习方法，解释性直接内嵌于算子结构本身，无需外部工具，有助于推动机器学习在科学应用中建立信任。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [88] [HERMES: A Multi-Granularity Labeling Substrate for Pre-training Data Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02266)
*Ziyun Qiao,Yue Min,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERMES是一个数据驱动的层次化标签系统，通过语义变换和残差向量量化将文档编码为粗到细的层次代码，解决了传统数据混合方法中标签系统固定粒度、不可复用的问题，并在1B参数、25B token的预训练中展示了不同粒度下混合策略效果的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合方法依赖于预先划分的固定标签系统（如来源、主题、格式或平面聚类），这些标签只在一个语义轴和一个粒度上表达，改变分辨率就需要重建标签。作者认为瓶颈在于标签系统本身而非混合器，因此需要一种可复用的、多粒度的层次化标签系统。

Method: HERMES包含两个核心组件：1）Learned Semantic Transform（学习语义变换）；2）3阶段残差向量量化（3-stage RVQ），将每个文档依次量化成一个粗到细的层次代码，前缀长度控制粒度，最多支持约13万个细胞单元。

Result: 在粗粒度上，HERMES在标准聚类指标上与KMeans类方法相当；在1B参数、25B token的预训练中，层次结构暴露了固定粒度无法测试的交互效应：在某一前缀长度下，结合Stage-2规则对比的混合策略将16任务能力宏观平均值提升+0.0253；在更细的下一级，该规则因候选池收缩约5倍而丧失可测量的优势。

Conclusion: HERMES将数据混合设计从在固定标签集中选择重新定义为在可复用的、数据驱动的粒度层次结构中导航，为更灵活、多分辨率的数据混合策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Most data-mixing methods assume the corpus has already been partitioned into groups, and the choice of those groups determines what a mixer can express. Existing labels, including provenance, topic or format taxonomies, and flat embedding clusters, commit to one semantic axis at one granularity; changing the resolution rebuilds the labels. We argue the bottleneck is the label system, not the mixer, and provide a hierarchical one. HERMES is a data-derived labeling substrate: a Learned Semantic Transform followed by 3-stage residual vector quantization annotates each document once into a coarse-to-fine code whose prefix length controls granularity up to approximately 130k cells. At coarse granularity HERMES sits at a plateau with KMeans-family methods on standard clustering metrics, so the contribution is the substrate, not the clusterer. On 1B-parameter, 25B-token pre-training, the hierarchy exposes an interaction fixed-granularity pipelines cannot test: at one prefix length, a combined Stage-2 rule contrast, equal-subbucket coverage versus size-proportional within-bucket quality top-30%, lifts a 16-task capability macro-average by +0.0253; at the next finer level, the same rule loses its measurable edge as candidate pools contract approximately 5x. HERMES reframes data mixture design from choosing among fixed label sets to navigating a reusable, data-derived granularity hierarchy.

</details>


### [89] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在离线强化学习的上下文MDP中，过度悲观本身不会阻碍最优泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的对称性；据此提出在策略提取阶段通过一致性损失应用数据增强，而非在常规离线训练中增强数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为离线RL中过度悲观会阻碍泛化，本文旨在澄清悲观程度与泛化能力之间的关系，指出真正影响泛化的是悲观结构是否与最优解的对称性一致。

Method: 理论证明：轻度悲观非对称值函数可能比过度悲观对称值函数泛化更差；实践方法：在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失进行数据增强（DA），而非在常规离线训练中增强数据集，并在旋转对称reacher环境中用IQL和CQL验证。

Result: 理论结果表明悲观结构（对称性）比悲观程度更影响泛化；实验验证了在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失的DA方法优于传统DA做法。

Conclusion: 离线RL中过度悲观不会天然阻碍最优泛化，设计尊重问题对称性的悲观结构才是关键；数据增强的最佳应用时机是策略提取阶段而非训练阶段。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [90] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分布级奖励的微调框架，通过子集替换策略和RL优化模型合并系数，在提升生成模型质量的同时避免模式崩塌，在SiT和EDM2上显著改善FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习策略使用样本级奖励函数会导致奖励黑客行为，降低图像多样性并引入视觉异常，需要一种分布级奖励机制来更好地对齐真实数据分布。

Method: 1) 使用分布级奖励替代样本级奖励，缓解模式崩塌问题；2) 提出子集替换策略高效计算奖励信号；3) 应用RL优化后验模型合并系数，解决SDE引入的训练-推理不一致。

Result: SiT模型FID-50K从8.30降至5.77，EDM2模型从3.74降至3.52；定性评估显示感知质量提升且多样性保持。

Conclusion: 分布级奖励微调框架有效解决样本级奖励导致的模式崩塌问题，在提升生成质量的同时保持多样性，且计算效率可接受。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [91] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近端波函数优化(PWO)算法，将变分能量最小化视为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，采用信任域方法同时裁剪振幅通道的概率比变化和相位通道的相位增量，实现了无需显式矩阵求逆、样本复用的稳定优化，在自旋系统和大规模NQS（15亿参数RWKV-7模型）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经量子态(NQS)优化方法存在两难：Adam可扩展但忽略函数空间几何，随机重配置(SR)理论完备但计算昂贵且数值不稳定，尤其在大模型中难以应用。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提出兼具可扩展性与理论保证的优化算法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新表述为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，引入信任域优化思想，提出PWO算法：在振幅通道裁剪概率比变化，在相位通道裁剪相位增量，避免显式矩阵求逆，并支持样本跨多次更新复用。

Result: 在Ising和阻挫J1-J2自旋系统的一维和二维模型中，PWO在稳定性和壁钟收敛速度上优于Adam、minSR和SPRING；成功微调了15亿参数的RWKV-7模型，将NQS优化规模提升了三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: PWO融合了一阶优化的可扩展性与信任域方法的理论保障，是NQS训练的有效替代方案，尤其适用于大规模模型，展示了NQS优化在远超此前工作规模上的可行性。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [92] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，一种即插即用的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵与输入相关残差组件替代标准自注意力中的查询-键投影，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时显著提升时间序列预测的推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力机制在时间序列预测中具有二次时间和内存复杂度（相对于回溯长度），这在资源受限或高吞吐量预测系统中限制了其应用。此外，时间序列数据中自注意力图往往存在跨时间步的冗余模式（源于重复的时间模式和稳定的时间相关性），这为设计更高效的注意力机制提供了机会。

Method: 提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，将注意力分数分解为两部分：一个共享的可学习矩阵（捕获常见注意力模式）和一个输入相关的残差组件（捕获输入依赖的变化）。SGA避免了标准注意力中的查询和键投影，实现了线性时间和分数矩阵内存复杂度（相对于回溯长度），并可作为即插即用模块集成到多种预测骨干网络中。

Result: 在覆盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动、气候记录等领域的9个公开真实数据集上，将SGA集成到多个预测骨干网络中，与标准自注意力和轻量级注意力变体进行比较。结果表明SGA在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA是一种高效的注意力替代方案，通过利用时间序列中的冗余注意力模式，在推理效率和预测精度之间取得了良好的平衡，为部署导向的时间序列预测系统提供了有价值的证据。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [93] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块，并通过重新组合子模块实现指数级候选解空间扩展，相比标准采样和RL方法，在相同GPU预算下显著提升代码生成任务的成功率，节约约50倍GPU令牌成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重复采样和强化学习方法在基础策略生成正确解的概率接近零时均失效——采样面临搜索空间过大的问题，RL则牺牲了样本多样性。作者希望从根本上改变策略：不"更努力地采样"，而是"让任务变简单"——通过问题分解来缩小搜索空间。

Method: DecompRL是一种强化学习算法，专门训练LLM学习将问题分解为层次化的代码结构并分别实现各子模块。通过重组k个n模块的实现，可获得k^n个候选解，将计算瓶颈从昂贵的GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests基准上（使用Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5令牌的预算下优于标准和多样性优化的RL基线，能够解决标准生成方法无法到达的问题，同时GPU令牌成本降低约50倍。

Conclusion: 问题分解与模块化重组是一种比单纯增加采样或优化单一准确率更有效的策略，尤其适用于基础策略几乎无法生成正确答案的困难问题，通过将计算从GPU转移到CPU实现了效率和可扩展性的显著提升。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [94] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuFS是一种基于神经元感知的主动少样本学习框架，通过利用模型内部神经元激活模式而非输出级信号来选择和标注最有价值的样本，在推理和文本分类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFSL方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、外部嵌入的语义相似性）来选择样本，忽略了模型的内部动态，无法精确定位模型的知识缺口，导致样本选择不够有效。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式直接表示样本，并采用双标准选择策略：(1) 通过神经元模式确保少样本多样性以覆盖更广泛的示例；(2) 通过量化神经元共识来优先识别信息量大且具有挑战性（LLM易产生幻觉）的样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务上均优于现有AFSL基线。消融研究显示内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供了更原则性且更有效的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS通过引入神经元感知的样本选择范式，证明了从输出级代理转向模型内部动态在主动少样本学习中的优越性，为更有效的样本选择提供了新方向。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [95] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QFedAgent，一种混合量子-经典联邦学习框架，用于多智能体活动识别。通过变分量子电路融合模块建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数（相比经典MLP融合的33K参数），实现约10倍参数缩减。在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，非独立同分布划分下达到97.7%平均测试准确率，验证了参数高效的量子融合与经典联邦基线相比具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习适用于隐私保护的机器人感知，但多智能体系统产生的异构非独立同分布多模态传感器数据会降低传统联邦学习算法性能，同时经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，集成变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数，相比经典MLP融合的33K参数实现约10倍参数缩减。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，基于主体的非独立同分布划分下，达到97.7%的平均测试准确率。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合模块与经典联邦基线相比具有竞争力，验证了混合量子-经典方法在多模态联邦学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Exformer框架，通过极值自适应注意力机制（包含Local、Stride、Extreme三个稀疏组件）来显式建模时间序列中的常规事件与极端事件，在四个真实水文数据集上取得了优于基线模型的3日径流预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中，罕见但关键的极端事件常被统一建模所忽视，尤其是在水文预测领域，极端洪峰对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统至关重要，而现有Transformer模型均匀对待所有时间点，难以充分表征极端模式。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏注意力组件：Local（捕获短期依赖）、Stride（捕获周期性依赖）和Extreme（选择性建模常规与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3日预测任务中均优于现有最先进的基线模型，证明了显式引入极端感知注意力能提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列上的预测能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模极端事件感知的注意力机制能有效提升Transformer模型在包含罕见但重要极端事件的不平衡时间序列上的预测性能。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [97] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非IID数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异质数据更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。基于理论分析，提出了MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论发现及MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习面临数据异质性的关键挑战，而现有研究缺乏对不同D-SSL框架在此挑战下鲁棒性的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过严格理论分析，对比了MIM和CL在非IID设置下的鲁棒性，分析了网络连通性对鲁棒性的影响；基于理论发现，设计了带有局部到全局对齐正则化的MAR损失函数。

Result: 理论结果表明MIM预训练本质上比CL对异质数据更鲁棒，且D-SSL的鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而增强（FL不低于DecL）；实验验证了理论发现及MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，MAR损失作为理论分析的应用实例进一步验证了理论的实际指导意义。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [98] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用内部神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略自蒸馏实现无标注微调，在提升领域内性能的同时保持跨域泛化并缓解校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（如SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的在线策略RL）存在域外性能下降或校准误差膨胀问题，亟需一种无需真实标签、专家标注或在线交互反馈的LLM后训练方法。

Method: Neuron-OPSD：利用内部神经元激活进行数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏从教师分布中训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持跨域泛化能力，并且缓解了校准误差膨胀问题，优于现有无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD是一种数据驱动的无标注自蒸馏框架，特别适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，与依赖已标注轨迹的离线RL方法有本质区别。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与传统Adam优化器在训练机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）模型（NequIP和Allegro）时的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其是在部分力监督条件下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs已成为AI科学模拟的重要方法，但社区默认使用Adam及其变体作为优化器，优化器选择对模型训练的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 实现了Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon三类矩阵结构优化器，并在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上进行系统对比实验，评估其与Adam在收敛速度和最终精度上的差异。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；Muon仅部分优于Adam；在部分力监督条件下，改进效果尤为明显。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，SOAP系列优化器是替代Adam的有力候选。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [100] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD提出了一种选择性采纳教师引导的自蒸馏框架，通过将学生导向教师与学生分布的逆向KL重心目标，解决了特权信息泄露和探索能力抑制问题，在科学推理任务上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法中，教师基于特权信息的密集token级监督会导致过拟合、抑制探索、损害跨领域泛化，并引发更根本的特权信息泄露问题——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案相关捷径。

Method: DemoPSD不拟合完整教师分布，而是将学生导向逆向KL重心目标（教师与学生分布的加权几何组合），通过测量两分布差异自适应控制每个token位置的混合权重，实现教师引导的选择性采纳。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域及OOD的GPQA基准上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持更高训练熵和更鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过选择性采纳教师引导，理论上实现了泄露衰减和探索保持，实验上验证了其有效性和泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [101] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"模糊函数编程"范式，通过PAW方法将自然语言规范编译为轻量级神经可执行程序，在本地高效运行，用1/50的推理内存达到32B模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常编程任务（如日志告警、JSON修复、搜索排序）难以用规则实现，目前依赖LLM API调用，导致延迟高、不可复现且成本昂贵。作者希望将这类任务从在线API调用转变为本地可执行的轻量级神经程序。

Method: 提出Program-as-Weights (PAW)框架，包含：(1) 一个4B参数的编译器，在10M样本的FuzzyBench数据集上训练；(2) 编译器输出参数高效适配器（LoRA等），用于冻结的轻量级解释器模型（0.6B Qwen3）；(3) 解释器执行这些适配器权重作为"程序"完成具体任务。

Result: 0.6B Qwen3解释器执行PAW程序，性能与直接提示32B Qwen3模型相当，但推理内存仅为后者的1/50，在MacBook M3上可达30 tokens/s的运行速度。

Conclusion: PAW将基础模型从"每次输入都推理"的问题求解器，转变为"一次性编译、多次廉价执行"的工具构建器，实现了本地化、低成本和可复现的模糊函数编程。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [HYPIC: Accelerating Hybrid-Attention LLM Serving with Position-Independent Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01299)
*Yifei Liu,Juntong Wu,Yang Liu,Junhao Hu,Minghao Li,Xiaoxu Chen,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hypic是首个支持混合注意力大语言模型与位置无关缓存的服务系统。通过提出段累积转移算子（segment-cumulative transition operator）实现线性注意力层的常量时间状态组合，利用段边界拼接窗口重计算修复全注意力层的跨段回溯，并利用段级自包含性并行化缓存缺失预填充，相比现有系统将TTFT平均降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐提升2.0倍，精度损失在3.3点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG和智能体LLM服务中，提示词由独立片段组装成长上下文，导致预填充阶段主导每请求计算成本。位置无关缓存（PIC）允许跨请求共享非连续片段的KV缓存，混合注意力模型用线性注意力替换大部分全注意力层以降低复杂度，但两者无法共存：PIC的逐token缓存复用原语不适用于混合注意力模型中的逐请求循环状态。

Method: 1) 在线性注意力层，识别段累积转移算子为缺失的代数原语，将其与每段的零起点终状态一起缓存，实现独立缓存段的近精确常量时间状态组合。2) 在全注意力层，发现注意力偏差集中在段边界，仅重计算每个边界处的小拼接窗口即可恢复跨段回溯。3) 利用段级自包含性，将缓存缺失的预填充跨实例并行化，将长冷请求转化为可加速的工作负载。

Result: 在四种混合注意力模型和五种工作负载上，Hypic相比现有系统将TTFT平均降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐提升2.0倍，精度保持在全重计算准确率的3.3点以内。

Conclusion: Hypic成功将位置无关缓存与混合注意力模型结合，通过识别线性注意力的代数原语、针对边界拼接窗口重计算全注意力层、以及并行化预填充，显著降低了TTFT并提升了吞吐量，同时保持高精度。

Abstract: In retrieval augmented generation (RAG) and agentic LLM serving, prompts are assembled from independent segments into long contexts, making the prefill stage dominate the per-request computation cost. To this cost, two directions have emerged in parallel: position-independent caching (PIC) admits KV reuse for non-contiguous segments shared across different requests, while hybrid-attention models reduce computation complexity by replacing most full-attention layers with linear attention. However, they cannot coexist: applying PIC to hybrid-attention models breaks down because per-token KV-cache reuse primitives do not transfer to the per-request recurrent state.
  In this work, we present Hypic, the first serving system for hybrid-attention LLMs with position-independent caching. For linear-attention layers, we identify the segment-cumulative transition operator as the missing algebraic primitive, and cache it alongside each segment's zero-start end-state, enabling near-exact and constant-time state composition of independently cached segments. For the remaining full-attention layers, existing PIC methods also fail as linear layers do not expose the per-token hidden states for selective recomputation. We show that the most significant attention deviation concentrates at segment boundaries, so recomputing only a small seam window at each boundary suffices to restore cross-segment lookback. Finally, Hypic exploits segment-level self-containment to parallelize cache-miss prefill across instances, turning long cold requests -- a major tail-latency contributor under both prefix caching and prior PIC -- into an accelerable workload. Evaluated across four hybrid-attention models and five workloads, Hypic reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 2.45x on average and improves peak throughput by up to 2.0x over existing systems, while staying within 3.3 points of full-recompute accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [SLFS: a Flexible, Low-Cost Distributed File System Using Serverless Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01486)
*Cheng Hao,Yang,Paola Alsharabaty,Soufiane Jounaid,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Ji-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SLFS 是首个基于无服务器函数构建的分布式文件系统，同时支持数据和元数据操作。它通过多线程短生命周期服务器设计克服冷启动问题，利用策略驱动协调器实现弹性伸缩，相比传统方案可降低最高68%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式文件系统按峰值需求配置资源，但文件访问模式波动大，导致非高峰时段大量容量闲置。现有弹性机制以整个服务器为粒度且耗时数分钟，无法跟踪文件系统常见的快速细粒度负载变化。

Method: SLFS 基于键值存储实现文件服务，保持函数操作简短简单；提出多线程短生命周期服务器设计克服冷启动问题同时保持低成本；使用策略驱动的协调器高效映射文件到函数实例、弹性伸缩系统、控制函数生命周期以平衡性能与成本。

Result: SLFS 将冷启动相比基础无服务器设计降低580倍，相比 λFS、EFS 和 Ceph，成本分别降低最多63%、68%和63%，同时性能更优。

Conclusion: 无服务器计算为分布式文件系统提供了极具吸引力的弹性方案，SLFS 证明了通过精心设计可以克服无服务器范式的冷启动等挑战，在保持高性能的同时显著降低运营成本。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed file systems must provision resources for peak demand, yet file access patterns fluctuate significantly, leaving substantial capacity idle during off-peak periods. Existing scaling mechanisms operate at the granularity of entire servers and take minutes to hours, making them unable to track the rapid, fine-grained load variations that file systems commonly experience. Serverless computing, with its millisecond-granularity elasticity and pay-per-use pricing, offers a compelling alternative. We present SLFS, the first distributed file system built with serverless functions for both data and metadata operations. SLFS implements file services on top of key-value stores, keeping function operations simple and short, and introduces a novel multi-threaded, short-lived server design that overcomes the cold-start problem while maintaining low cost. A policy-enforcing coordinator efficiently maps files to function instances, scales the system elastically, and controls function lifetimes to balance performance and cost. SLFS can flexibly run on diverse storage backends -- from cloud-native services like S3 to user-managed key-value stores -- enabling configurable cost-performance trade-offs. Our evaluation shows that SLFS mitigates cold starts by 580$\times$ compared to the base serverless design and outperforms $λ$FS, EFS, and Ceph at up to 63%, 68%, and 63% lower cost, respectively.

</details>


### [104] [OmniPilot: An Uncertainty-Aware LLM Inference Advisor for Heterogeneous GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01579)
*D. Balamurugan,Thomas W. Bush*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OmniPilot 是一个面向异构 GPU 集群的 LLM 服务启动顾问，通过校准分位数成本模型和 OOD 弃权层，在 460 次基准测试中实现 6.2% 的吞吐量 MAPE 和 0.003 的效用遗憾，并能有效识别低置信度配置请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享异构 GPU 集群上部署 LLM 时，用户需要选择 GPU 类型、张量并行度和精度，但静态配置方案忽略了量化效果、KV 缓存压力与精度的权衡、故障率差异等关键交互，且集群资源常受硬件故障限制。

Method: OmniPilot 采用保形校准的分位数成本模型（覆盖八个服务目标）与 OOD 弃权层相结合，通过经济效用指标（根据操作者的显示偏好校准）对配置进行排序。

Result: 在 A100、H100、H200 硬件上跨越四种精度的 460 次基准测试中，聚合吞吐量 MAPE 为 6.2%，对数空间 R²=0.92，top-1 准确率 95%，平均效用遗憾 0.003；OOD 样本预测误差升至 24-46%，但弃权层成功标记全部五个为低置信度。

Conclusion: OmniPilot 通过预测成本与弃权机制有效指导 LLM 服务配置选择，且支持通过纳入 OOD 样本持续扩展支持范围。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) on a shared, heterogeneous GPU cluster requires users and operators to select the GPU type, tensor-parallel degree, and precision before committing valuable node-hours. Making these choices is challenging because effective throughput, launch-success rates, and cluster demand and utilization continuously fluctuate. Furthermore, static configuration recipes miss critical interactions: quantization effects depend heavily on the model family, key-value cache pressure creates size-by-precision trade-offs, and failure rates vary by more than twofold across different tensor-parallel degrees. Additionally, cluster resources are frequently constrained by unpredictable hardware failures. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{OmniPilot}, a launch advisor that predicts serving costs for feasible configurations and abstains when requests fall outside its measured support envelope. OmniPilot pairs a conformally calibrated quantile cost model (spanning eight serving targets) with an out-of-distribution (OOD) abstention layer. It ranks configurations using an economic utility metric calibrated to an operator's revealed preferences. In evaluations across 460 benchmark runs on A100, H100, and H200 hardware across four precisions, OmniPilot predicts aggregate throughput with a 6.2\% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) and a log-space $R^2=0.92$. The advisor achieves 95\% top-1 accuracy with a mean utility regret of just 0.003. When tested on an OOD holdout of unsupported cells, prediction error climbs to 24-46\% and conformal intervals cover 0 of 5 points; however, the abstention layer successfully flags all five as low-confidence. Over time, these OOD scenarios will be integrated into the training dataset to continuously expand the advisor's support envelope.

</details>


### [105] [Arachne: Orchestrating Cascades for Efficient Text-to-Video Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01701)
*Peng Yu,Yuankai Fan,Yang Qiu,Tian Li,Bihuan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Arachne是一个针对大规模文本到视频（T2V）模型训练的高效框架。它通过将训练过程分解为细粒度计算单元（cascades），并协调空间和时间维度的分布式执行与同步，解决了传统bucketing策略在静态并行方案下导致的负载不均和硬件利用率低的问题。实验表明，Arachne相比主流框架可减少高达65%的迭代时间，且性能优势随训练规模扩大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模T2V模型训练采用bucketing策略处理异构视频数据（不同分辨率和时长），但在数据与序列并行等静态并行方案下，随着计算和数据规模增长，该策略难以扩展，导致严重的工作负载不均衡和硬件利用率低下。

Method: 提出Arachne框架，将训练过程分解为细粒度计算单元（称为cascades），并通过协调的空间和时间优化来编排这些单元在集群中的分布式执行与同步。

Result: 在全面评估中，Arachne相比主流框架迭代时间减少高达65%，且展现出正向扩展趋势，其性能优势随训练规模增大而更加显著。

Conclusion: Arachne通过细粒度计算分解和时空协同优化，有效解决了大规模T2V模型训练中的负载不均衡问题，实现了更高效的分布式训练，尤其在大规模场景下优势明显。

Abstract: The rising demand for AI-generated videos is fueled by advances in large-scale Text-to-Video (T2V) models, trained on extensive datasets of video clips spanning diverse resolutions and durations. To address this data heterogeneity, current training methods often use a bucketing strategy that groups samples into discrete buckets for efficiency. However, this approach struggles to scale with compute and data volumes under static parallelism schemes, such as data and sequence parallelism, leading to significant workload imbalances and hardware under-utilization.
  In this paper, we present Arachne, a novel training framework for efficient T2V model training at scale. Arachne decomposes the training process into fine-grained computational units, called \textit{cascades}, orchestrating their distributed execution and synchronization across the cluster through coordinated spatial and temporal optimization. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Arachne reduces iteration time by up to 65\% over leading frameworks, exhibiting a positive scaling trend where its performance advantages amplify as training scale grows.

</details>


### [106] [Exploiting Task-Based Parallelism for the Red-Black Gauss-Seidel Method on 2D Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01735)
*Shiting Long,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo,Andreas Frommer,Dirk Pleiter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于任务编程模型实现红黑高斯-赛德尔迭代法，相比传统分治并行策略，任务并行方法能消除全局同步、实现细粒度并行，在2D泊松方程求解中达到相近性能且对硬件异步性更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多色高斯-赛德尔迭代使用分治并行策略存在全局同步开销和负载不平衡问题，影响并行效率。

Method: 采用两种任务编程模型实现红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，并与经典分治并行实现进行对比，以2D泊松方程求解为基准测试。

Result: 任务并行实现可达到与传统分治并行相当的性能，同时对硬件级异步性具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 任务编程模型能有效消除全局同步、实现细粒度并行，可自然扩展到非结构化网格和宽模板的更一般多色方案。

Abstract: Gauss-Seidel is a well-established iterative method for the solution of linear systems, and multicoloring has been widely used to increase parallelism in iterative solution techniques. Implementing multi-color Gauss-Seidel with conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization strategies, however, may be inefficient due to global synchronization requirements and load imbalances. Task-based programming models can mitigate these issues by enabling fine-grained parallelism, removing global barriers and allowing updates of different colors to partially overlap in time. In this work, we implement the red-black Gauss-Seidel method using two task-based programming models and compare them with a classical divide-and-conquer parallel implementation to evaluate the impact of fine-grained parallelism on execution efficiency. The red-black scheme serves as a representative example, as task-based approaches naturally extend to more general multi-color schemes arising from unstructured grids and wider stencils. Using the solve of the 2D Poisson equation as benchmark, our results show that task-based implementations can achieve performance comparable to conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization while providing greater resilience to hardware-level asynchronicity.

</details>


### [107] [HCMS: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline for Communication-Computation Overlap in Long-Sequence Parallel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01817)
*Chao Yuan,Pan Li,Yingnan Sun,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HCMS通过将多头注意力中的注意力头分块，并利用双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠，在不修改底层内核的情况下，对中长序列处理实现10%-17.5%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于全连接的序列并行方法在处理中长序列时，通信和计算严格串行执行，导致硬件资源利用率低下。

Method: 提出Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS)，利用多头注意力的计算独立性，将注意力头划分为多个块，通过双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠。

Result: 在4种GPU平台、2-8 GPU规模上，对于31K-56K tokens的视频生成序列，HCMS相比Ulysses基线加速10%-17.5%，相比Ring Attention加速5%-14.5%；在Wan2.2模型上实现6.8%端到端加速。

Conclusion: HCMS的收益与通信比率ρ正相关，建议在ρ>20%时使用HCMS。

Abstract: All-to-all based sequence parallelism methods execute communication and computation strictly in serial when processing medium-long sequences, resulting in hardware resource underutilization. This paper proposes Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS), which exploits the computational independence of multi-head attention by partitioning attention heads into multiple chunks and achieving fine-grained communication-computation overlap through dual CUDA streams. HCMS is orthogonally compatible with existing optimizations such as FlashAttention and SDPA, requires no modification to underlying kernels, supports uneven partitioning while maintaining numerical equivalence. Experiments validate the effectiveness across four GPU platforms at 2-8 GPU scales: for typical video generation sequence lengths of 31K-56K tokens, HCMS achieves 10\%-17.5\% speedup over the Ulysses baseline and 5\%-14.5\% speedup over Ring Attention; end-to-end acceleration of 6.8\% is achieved on the Wan2.2 model. Theoretical analysis shows that HCMS benefits are positively correlated with communication ratio $ρ$, and its use is recommended when $ρ>20\%$.

</details>


### [108] [Lynx: Progressive Speculative Quantization for accelerating KV Transfer in Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01831)
*Wenchen Han,Gingfung Matthew Yeung,Marco Barletta,William Toner,Amory Hoste,Adam Barker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lynx系统通过将KV缓存划分为高优先级Anchor流（承载最高有效位）和低优先级Residual流（承载剩余精度）实现渐进式分流传榆，解码可在收到Anchor流后立即开始，同时并发传输Residual流，随后进行验证以确保等价于高精度解码。相比标准8位KV量化，TTFT提升最高1.43倍；相比最先进方法，准确率提升最高5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，分离式架构需要在网络间传输大型KV缓存，解码必须等待传输完成才能开始，造成网络暴露延迟。现有KV量化方案无法同时实现低网络暴露延迟和高推理精度。

Method: 挑战KV缓存必须完整接收才能使用的假设，利用KV缓存中不同比特对注意力计算贡献不均的性质（最高有效位捕捉粗粒度注意力结构，最低有效位细化精度），将KV缓存划分为高优先级Anchor流和低优先级Residual流进行渐进式分流传榆，解码收到Anchor流后立即开始推测性执行，同时并发传输Residual流，最后通过验证保证等价性。

Result: TTFT与激进4位KV量化相当，精度匹配高精度BF16推理；相比标准8位KV量化TTFT提升1.43倍；相比最先进方法准确率提升5.1%。

Conclusion: 通过渐进式KV缓存传输和解码策略，Lynx成功打破了KV缓存必须完整传输后才能解码的瓶颈，在网络延迟和推理精度之间取得了更好的权衡。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly common in large language model (LLM) serving, driven by retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems. In disaggregated inference, these workloads require transferring large Key-Value (KV) caches across the network, where decoding cannot begin until the transfer completes. Recent KV quantization techniques reduce data volume and alleviate this bottleneck, but existing schemes fail to achieve both low network-exposed latency and high inference accuracy.
  We challenge the assumption that the KV cache is an indivisible unit that must be fully received before use. We leverage the observation that different bits in the KV cache contribute unequally to attention computation and inference precision: the most significant bits capture the coarse structure of attention and the least significant bits refine precision. This property enables partial use of the KV cache during decoding.
